TC-SEATMAP-POS-02 - Verify GetSeatMap sets getSeatMapOfferId/getSeatMapResponceID and Book consumes them as ResponseId/selectedOfferId when SeatMap precedes itBLOCKED
Endpoint Name: Confirm the seat-map step hands a usable reference to the booking step, and that booking accepts it
Request: Searched a one-way flight from Bahrain to Riyadh for one adult, opened the seat map, then tried to book using the seat map's own reference (no seat chosen) — three separate times
Response: The seat map opened every time with a valid reference; every booking attempt ended in an error after about 35 seconds and returned no booking reference
Expected: The booking should be accepted and return its booking reference and ticket details
Actual: Could not be judged. The system did create real tickets each time (booking references RIPLBZ, TVQBBV and RCEVKA were found inside the error messages) but never told the caller. This is the already-known BUG-16 pattern and it blocks this check
Status: BLOCKED
Evidence
3 of 3 Book calls 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999 / SocketException; confirmation e-mail inside each envelope shows a real booking: RIPLBZ, TVQBBV (ticket 722136996973), RCEVKA (ticket 722136996974), each 993.00 SAR; GetSeatMap responseId a87b75c4-8562-4de9-88ec-6ac2947e38a0 == AddPassenger responseId a87b75c4-8562-4de9-88ec-6ac2947e38a0

  1. AddPassenger -> GetSeatMap (send ResponseId/selectedOfferId = AddPassenger's IDs) -> capture resp.offerId/resp.responseId -> Book (send ResponseId=getSeatMapResponceID, selectedOfferId=getSeatMapOfferId).
Expected Result
GetSeatMap returns HTTP 200 with a non-empty offerId/responseId; the collection variables getSeatMapOfferId and getSeatMapResponceID (note the collection's own "Responce" spelling) are populated from these; Book accepts them and returns pnr/bookingToken/gdsPNR without needing AddPassenger's IDs re-sent -- this is a behavior change from before 2026-08-10, when GetSeatMap set no variables at all and Book still read AddPassenger's IDs.
Actual Result
GetSeatMap half confirmed (200, offerId/responseId non-empty and equal to the AddPassenger values). Book half could not be evaluated: Book accepted the IDs (it created real bookings RIPLBZ, TVQBBV, RCEVKA) but answered 409 with no pnr, so the 'returns pnr/bookingToken/gdsPNR' assertion has no response to check. Blocked by BUG-16 (Book silently succeeds while returning an error).

Actual Result
Not evaluable. The offer used here had already been silently booked by the POS-02 attempt (BUG-16), so Book answered 'booking already created' before looking at the seat, and a Book on a fresh offer would only have returned the same 409-without-pnr envelope, so there is no seat echo to check. No new booking was made for this case, to avoid piling up untracked live bookings. The cellID/cellId casing question stays CONFIRMED from Rounds 1-11 (case-insensitive binding); it was not re-tested.
TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04 - Validate a cellID obtained from one offer's GetSeatMap response is rejected when submitted in Book for a different offerFAIL
Endpoint Name: Confirm a seat code taken from one flight offer is refused when used on a different offer
Request: Opened seat maps for two separate offers, then tried to book on the second offer with seat 12B but using the seat code copied from the first offer's seat 12C
Response: After about 48 seconds the booking call ended in the general error (BUG-16), but the error message contains a real booking confirmation: reference REGOSM, ticket 722136996975, total 1053 SAR
Expected: The booking should have been refused straight away, because the seat code belongs to another offer
Actual: BUG-07, 8th confirmed occurrence (not new) — the foreign seat code was not rejected; a real ticketed booking was created with the 60 SAR seat added to the price (993 + 60 = 1053 SAR), and the seat then disappeared from the seat maps
Status: FAIL
Evidence
booking REGOSM, ticket 722136996975, fare 640.00 + 353.00 + seat 60.00 = 1053.00; seat 12B: AvailablePaid before, NoSeat on Offer B re-read and on a fresh Offer D afterwards (see TC-SEATMAP-BIZ-04); an earlier, faster 'already created' reject (1.4s) on a previously-used offer is NOT evidence and is ignored

View Test Case
Preconditions
Two independent, completed Search->FareConfirm->AddPassenger->GetSeatMap chains (Offer A and Offer B) for the same supplier/credentialsSelector.
Steps
  1. Chain A: Search->FareConfirm->AddPassenger->GetSeatMap, record cellID_A. Chain B: Search->FareConfirm->AddPassenger->GetSeatMap, obtain getSeatMapResponceID_B/getSeatMapOfferId_B. Book using Chain B's ResponseId/selectedOfferId but passengersSelectedSeats.cellId=cellID_A.
Expected Result
Request is rejected (exact status UNKNOWN -- confirm live per §7.9), since cellID appears (per its base64-decoded contents) to embed the originating ResponseId/OfferId/passenger-ref metadata and is presumably bound to the offer it was issued for.
Actual Result
STILL REPRODUCES (BUG-07, 8th confirmed occurrence). The foreign cellId was not rejected: Book ran the full supplier round-trip and created a real, ticketed booking with seat 12B priced in (total 1053.00 SAR = 993.00 + 60.00). The Book response itself was the Book-wide 409 (BUG-16), so this verdict rests on the booking record embedded in the envelope plus seat 12B disappearing from later seat maps, not on a 200 echo.
TC-SEATMAP-VAL-07 - Verify window/aisle/extra-legroom/exit-row seat attributes are programmatically derivable from cellCharacteristics for every Seat-category cellPASS
Endpoint Name: Confirm each seat's window, aisle, extra-legroom and exit-row attributes can be worked out from the seat map data
Request: Opened the seat map for one adult on Bahrain to Riyadh (1 July 2027) and checked all 176 seats
Response: The seat map returned 176 seats in 30 rows; every seat carries descriptive text but none carries a standard airline seat-characteristic code
Expected: Window, aisle, legroom and exit-row attributes should be derivable and never contradict a seat's column position
Actual: All 176 seats are consistent: 59 window, 59 aisle, 58 middle, 24 exit-row seats (rows 18–21) and 16 extra-legroom seats. The attributes can only be read from the description text, because the standard codes are all "Unknown" for this airline
Status: PASS
Evidence
column/description mismatches: 0 of 176; WINDOW=59, AISLE_SEAT=59, CENTER_SEAT=58; EXIT_ROW_SEAT=24 (rows 18-21); LEG_SPACE=16 (rows 8,19,20); BULKHEAD=4; padisCode Unknown=797/797

View Test Case
Preconditions
GetSeatMap completed for a confirmed offer with a populated seatMapDetails.
Steps
  1. GetSeatMap -> for one segment, collect every rowNumber (check uniqueness), every zoneId per deck (check uniqueness within that deck), and every cell's column value (check domain membership).
Expected Result
Automatable assertion: no rowNumber repeats within the segment; no zoneId repeats within the same deck; every non-null column value is one of A/B/C/D/E/F (per the observed 6-column-per-row layout, SPEC.md §2.7); null column values correspond only to Aisle-category cells (cross-ref TC-SEATMAP-VAL-06).
Actual Result
All four constraints hold: 30 row numbers, 30 unique; 1 zone in deck Main (unique); Seat-cell columns are exactly {A,B,C,D,E,F}; the 30 null-column cells are all Aisle-category. The 4 NoSeat cells (8A, 8E, 10B, 10C) carry a real column rather than null - allowed by the rule (it only restricts nulls). Every row deck/zoneId resolves to the declared layout. Unchanged from Rounds 1-11.

View Test Case
Preconditions
GetSeatMap completed for a multi-passenger offer with a populated seatMapDetails.
Steps
  1. GetSeatMap -> for a sample of cells, compare cellAvailability (overall) against the set of passenger refs actually present in seatPriceDetailsByPassenger for that cell -> cross-check each listed passenger's presence against passengersSeatMapAvailability.<paxRef>.<segmentRefId> (the separate top-level per-passenger cellCode allow-list, also confirmed present in the same saved example).
Expected Result
Automatable assertion (structural, not value-equality, and NOT a completeness requirement): a passenger's absence from a cell's seatPriceDetailsByPassenger is EXPECTED and valid whenever that passenger is not eligible for that cell (confirmed pattern: a CHD/INF passenger restricted from a forward/exit cell won't appear there, per this same example's passengersSeatMapAvailability showing PAX3/PAX4 with a much shorter or empty allow-list than PAX1/PAX2). Do NOT assert every passenger must appear per priced cell -- only assert internal consistency: a passenger listed in seatPriceDetailsByPassenger for a cell SHOULD also have that cell's cellCode present in their own passengersSeatMapAvailability entry (if that top-level structure is present in the response). Do not assert cellAvailability == a per-passenger equivalent for all passengers either, since SeatMap QC Execution Checklist.md §11 explicitly notes these may differ legitimately (e.g. a seat blocked for one passenger type but not another).
Actual Result
Internally consistent: 528 passenger-cell price entries (176 x 3), 0 listed for a cell absent from that passenger's allow-list, 0 allow-list codes without a priced cell. GulfAir lists all three passengers on every cell (so the 'absence is valid' branch could not be exercised - fine, it is not required), and per-passenger paxCellAvailability equals the overall cellAvailability everywhere (0 differences; all AvailablePaid). For context: the CHD passenger is offered every exit-row cell (24/24), the already-tracked BUG-09.
TC-SEATMAP-BIZ-04 - Exploratory - confirm GetSeatMap reflects real-time supplier inventory rather than a cached snapshotPASS
Endpoint Name: Confirm the seat map shows live seat availability rather than a stored copy
Request: Opened the seat map (seat 12B available), then seat 12B was taken by a booking, then opened the same offer's seat map again and a brand-new offer's seat map
Response: Before: 12B available for 60 SAR. After: 12B is gone (shown as an empty cell) on both the same offer and the new offer; 176 seats became 175
Expected: Unknown — if a seat can be taken in between, the map should change, proving it is live
Actual: Confirmed live: the change showed up immediately on both offers. The booking that took the seat reported an error (BUG-16) but still went through. A taken seat is shown as an empty "no seat" cell, not as "Occupied"
Status: PASS
Evidence
Row 12 seat B: AvailablePaid (call #1) -> NoSeat/null (calls #2 and #3); seat cells 176 -> 175; NoSeat cells 4 -> 5; PAX1 allow-list contains 12B before, not after; response headers carry no Cache-Control/ETag (CorrelationId only)
Hops (4)

View Test Case
Preconditions
A test environment/mechanism that can independently occupy a seat between the two GetSeatMap calls (may require a second tester/session or coordination with the team -- environment-dependent, same caveat class as TC-BOOK-NEG-09).
Steps
  1. GetSeatMap call #1 (record a specific cell's cellAvailability) -> independently occupy/select that same cell via a separate booking action -> GetSeatMap call #2 (same offer/segment) -> compare the cell's cellAvailability between the two calls.
Expected Result
UNKNOWN -- resolve empirically; if reliably forceable, expect the cell's cellAvailability to transition (e.g. AvailablePaid -> Occupied) between the two calls, confirming a live supplier round-trip rather than a cached response. If not reliably forceable in this environment, downgrade to logging response timing/headers (e.g. absence of a Cache-Control/ETag suggesting no caching layer) as weaker circumstantial evidence instead.
Actual Result
CONFIRMED live inventory, again (first confirmed Round 11). The seat was taken by a booking whose Book response was a 409 (BUG-16), but the booking really existed at the supplier, and both the same-offer and a fresh-offer seat map dropped seat 12B straight away. A taken seat is still NOT reported as cellAvailability 'Occupied': the cell becomes cellCategory 'NoSeat' with cellDetails null and leaves passengersSeatMapAvailability.
🔒Security0 passed1 failed
TC-SEATMAP-SEC-01 - Apply the three universal caller-facing security checks (invalid x-api-key, invalid Client-Id, cross-tenant supplier/credentialsSelector) to GetSeatMapFAIL
Endpoint Name: Confirm the seat-map request is refused for a wrong key, a wrong client name, or another tenant's credentials
Request: Sent the seat-map request four ways: wrong access key, wrong client name, a different airline system, and the same airline with another country's credentials (UAE instead of KSA) — plus three extra checks
Response: Wrong key: refused. Wrong client name: refused. Different airline system: refused. Same airline with UAE credentials: accepted — the full seat map came back
Expected: All three kinds of request should be refused
Actual: BUG-17, 2nd confirmed occurrence (not new) — switching only the credentials country is not checked, so an offer created under one tenant (KSA) can be read through another (UAE)
Status: FAIL
Evidence
control 200; badKey 401 AuthenticationError "Invalid authorization header"; badClient 401 (same envelope); supplier switch 400 NDC-INT-TEC-A0001 SelectedOfferId "Failed to deserialize the selected offer"; GulfAir/UAE with a KSA offer 200 (~198 KB body, offerId/responseId returned, responseId equal to the KSA offer's)
Control - valid headers, GulfAir/KSA (baseline)

View Test Case
Preconditions
A valid AddPassenger-derived ResponseId/selectedOfferId pair; a second supplier/credentialsSelector pair available for the cross-tenant variant.
Steps
  1. Run (1) with a corrupted x-api-key. Run (2) with a corrupted Client-Id. Run (3) with valid headers but a different supplier/credentialsSelector than the one used to establish this ResponseId/selectedOfferId.
Expected Result
All three rejected (exact status/error shape UNKNOWN, confirm live and reuse whatever envelope the same three checks produce on other endpoints, §7.14, for consistency).
Actual Result
Two of the three checks pass cleanly, the third only half-passes. Switching to a different SUPPLIER is rejected (400), but switching only the credentialsSelector to another VALID one for the same supplier (GulfAir/UAE, a real GulfAir tenant) is NOT rejected: GetSeatMap returns the complete seat map for an offer created under KSA. So credentialsSelector is still not enforced as an authorization boundary on GetSeatMap (BUG-17, reproduced for the 2nd time), contradicting SPEC.md 7.14's description of supplier/credentialsSelector as the API's implicit authorization boundary.

View Test Case
Preconditions
N/A.
Steps
  1. Review SPEC.md §1.5/§1.8 (static, hardcoded x-api-key/Client-Id sent by THIS layer to the caller; supplier-side credentials are a separate, server-side-only concern not exposed in any request/response this suite can observe) and the PRD's §7 Non-Functional Requirements (file-only ApiResponse/SLF4J logger, correlation-ID tracing) for what actually governs supplier-auth and logging.
Expected Result
This case does not assert a pass/fail against the live API. It documents that: (a) User Story AC8's "securely authenticates with airline supplier APIs" describes THIS system's outbound credential handling toward suppliers -- a server-side/config concern, confirmable only via a backend credential-storage/config audit or the automation framework's own code review (PRD §7's Gap #1 already flags this as hardcoded tech debt), not via any request this Postman-collection-driven suite can send; (b) AC9's "provides logging" is confirmable only via log/observability review (PRD §7's ApiResponse logger, correlation-ID tracing), not via API response inspection. TC-SEATMAP-NEG-05 covers AC9's "handles errors gracefully" half, which IS API-observable.
Actual Result
Documented as specified; SPEC.md 1.5/1.8 and NDC_PRD.md 7 (Gap #1: hardcoded x-api-key/Client-Id) re-checked and still say the same. AC8/AC9 must not be marked covered by API tests without the paired backend/config/log audit. Unchanged from Rounds 1-11.
Findings3Defects0Unknown-resolution
Defect — BUG-16, 2nd confirmed occurrence (not new)
Book returns HTTP 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999 (SocketException "Name or service not known") after 34-48 seconds while the booking is actually created and ticketed: the envelope embeds the booking's confirmation e-mail, and re-submitting the same offer answers "This booking has already been created (609)". The caller never receives a pnr/bookingToken. (SPEC.md §2.9, TC-SEATMAP-POS-02)
Field: Book response (HTTP status / pnr / bookingToken)
Expected value: HTTP 200 with {"pnr":"<real PNR>","bookingToken":"<token>","gdsPNR":"<value or empty>"} - a booking that was created must be reported as created
History: First observed Round 8 (2026-09-09, as HTTP 500 + "already created (609)" on retry); not reproduced in Rounds 9, 10, 11 (Book returned a clean 200); reproduced again Round 12 (2026-09-30) on 4 of 4 fresh Book calls, now as HTTP 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999.
Impact: Real ticketed bookings are created with no identifier returned to the caller, so they cannot be tracked, voided or cancelled through the API; every Book-dependent test (POS-02, VAL-03) is blocked. New root-cause clue versus Round 8: the failure appears to be in the post-booking confirmation-e-mail step (DNS failure), not the supplier call. The failure envelopes also expose recipient contact details, PNR and ticket number. See full bug report.
Evidence: 4 of 4 fresh Book calls 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999 (34.0s, 34.2s, 36.9s, 47.7s) with Context SocketException "Name or service not known"; each envelope's embedded confirmation e-mail shows a real booking: RIPLBZ/722136996972, TVQBBV/722136996973, RCEVKA/722136996974, REGOSM/722136996975 (993.00 SAR; REGOSM 1053.00 SAR incl. seat); re-submits on used offers -> 409 "This booking has already been created (609)".
Defect — BUG-07, 8th confirmed occurrence (not new)
Book accepts a cellId issued for a different offer's GetSeatMap response and creates a ticketed booking with the seat attached and priced. (SPEC.md §2.7, §2.9, §7.7, TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04)
Field: passengersSelectedSeats.<paxRef>.setSegmentsSeats.<segmentRefId>.cellId
Expected value: 4xx validation error rejecting a cellId that does not belong to this offer, with no booking created
History: First observed Round 1 (2026-08-25); confirmed Rounds 1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7, 11 and now 12 (Round 4 blocked by an outage; Rounds 8-10 did not exercise it).
Impact: The cellId token is not validated at all; seat selection is driven purely by cellCode, so a stale, foreign or forged token still gets a caller charged and ticketed for the seat. This round the evidence comes from the booking record inside the BUG-16 envelope, not from a 200 echo. See full bug report.
Evidence: Book on Offer C (cellCode 12B) with Offer B's 12C cellId ran the full ~48s booking path and created booking REGOSM / ticket 722136996975, total 1053.00 SAR = 993.00 + 60.00 seat; seat 12B then disappeared from every later seat map.
Defect — BUG-17, 2nd confirmed occurrence (not new)
GetSeatMap does not reject a request whose credentialsSelector differs from the one the offer was created under (same supplier, different valid selector). (SPEC.md §7.14, TC-SEATMAP-SEC-01)
Field: credentialsSelector
Expected value: 4xx rejection (offer not found / not authorized for this credentialsSelector), as happens when the supplier is switched
History: First observed Round 11 (2026-09-29); reproduced again Round 12 (2026-09-30).
Impact: credentialsSelector is the API's per-tenant boundary (SPEC.md 7.14). A caller holding an offerId/responseId from one tenant (KSA) can read that offer's seat map through another tenant's selector (UAE), so cross-tenant isolation depends solely on offer IDs being unguessable. Other offer-consuming endpoints (Book, GetAncillary, FareCheck) were not tested. See full bug report.
Evidence: Offer created under GulfAir/KSA; GetSeatMap with GulfAir/UAE -> 200, full seat map (~198 KB, same responseId); supplier switched to TravelportNdc/UAE -> 400 NDC-INT-TEC-A0001; invalid key/Client-Id -> 401 AuthenticationError.

Also re-confirmed this round: BUG-11 (The "This booking has already been created (609)" 409 envelope again echoes the full upstream SOAP request including live apiKey/secretKey in plaintext (seen on both re-submitted Book calls; values not recorded here).) — see TC-SEATMAP-POS-02; BUG-09 (The CHD passenger (PAX3) is offered every exit-row seat (24 of 24 EXIT_ROW_SEAT cells in its allow-list).) — see TC-SEATMAP-BIZ-03.

Not reproduced

BUG-01 (Book with a seat selected never returns) — exercised by TC-SEATMAP-VAL-03, TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04. The only with-seat Book that ran to the supplier (VAL-04) returned a response after 47.7s (a BUG-16 409, not a hang); the VAL-03 attempt returned in 1.7s. Not proof of a fix: no with-seat Book returned a 200 this round.

Proposed SPEC.md updates0Proposed Updates

No SPEC.md updates proposed this run. Round 11's three pending proposals are unchanged; this run re-confirmed the first one (a taken seat becomes a NoSeat cell) on TC-SEATMAP-BIZ-04.

Cleanup / follow-up
Live artifacts left behind this run (4):
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR RIPLBZ (TC-SEATMAP-POS-02) — Real ticketed GulfAir/KSA booking (BAH-RUH 2027-07-01, 1 ADT, 993.00 SAR, ticket 722136996972, no seat) from the A - first POS-02 attempt (no seat). Created while Book answered 409 (BUG-16): the pnr was recovered from the confirmation e-mail inside the error envelope and no bookingToken was ever returned, so Void via the API may not be possible - ask the platform team to cancel it.
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR TVQBBV (TC-SEATMAP-POS-02) — Real ticketed GulfAir/KSA booking (BAH-RUH 2027-07-01, 1 ADT, 993.00 SAR, ticket 722136996973, no seat) from the A2 - POS-02 main attempt (no seat). Created while Book answered 409 (BUG-16): the pnr was recovered from the confirmation e-mail inside the error envelope and no bookingToken was ever returned, so Void via the API may not be possible - ask the platform team to cancel it.
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR RCEVKA (TC-SEATMAP-POS-02) — Real ticketed GulfAir/KSA booking (BAH-RUH 2027-07-01, 1 ADT, 993.00 SAR, ticket 722136996974, no seat) from the A3 - POS-02 repeat attempt (no seat). Created while Book answered 409 (BUG-16): the pnr was recovered from the confirmation e-mail inside the error envelope and no bookingToken was ever returned, so Void via the API may not be possible - ask the platform team to cancel it.
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR REGOSM (TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04) — Real ticketed GulfAir/KSA booking (BAH-RUH 2027-07-01, 1 ADT, 1053.00 SAR incl. seat 12B, ticket 722136996975). Evidence for BUG-07 - keep until triaged. pnr recovered from the BUG-16 error envelope; no bookingToken was returned.
